Algorithm:
- Choose n maybe 2535 or larger.
- Initialize p with central binomial prefix normalized to sum 1.
- For iteration:
  - Compute q=p*p via FFT/direct (length 2n-1). nonnegative.
  - Score=2n*max(q).
  - Select active weights w. Options:
    a) Smooth: w_k = softmax(q_k / temperature), but q values near max, others maybe small. Temperature small to focus on max. Use top fraction? Could set w_k = 1 for q_k >= max_q - ε, else 0, or soft kernel exp(α(q_k - max)). Also include near-boundary constraints to prevent overshoot? If only exact max plateau (n lags) central p, all left half active; that's good. If one coefficient slightly exceeds, reduce it. Softens.

Convolution via FFT:
```
def fft_convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); L=1<<(n+m-2).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:n+m-1]
    return c
```

Algorithm LP-based:
```

       p_cand = g/G
       # maybe blend: for t in [1,0.8,0.5,0.2]: p_t = normalize((1-t)*p + t*p_cand)

Update options detail:
```
def multiplicative_step(p, q, weights, alpha):
    w=weights
    h=coverage(p,w)
    hmax=h.max()
    if hmax <=0: return p
    # add floor = 1e-15 * hmax
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-15*hmax)
    logp=np.log(p + tiny)
    # target log_h = mean? To minimize variance of log h: center = mean logh (or median)
    target=np.mean(np.log(h))
    logp_new = (1-beta)*logp + beta*(target - np.log(h))
    # Or p *= exp(alpha*(target - logh))
    p_new=np.exp(logp_new)
    p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0)
    s=p_new.sum()
    if s==0: return p
    return p_new/s
```

```
def solve_lp(f):
    m=2*n-1
    # build A sparse
    A = build_conv_matrix(f)  # shape m x n
    res = linprog(c=-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=ones, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-10, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-10})
    return res.x if success
```

3. `def normalize_nonneg(a):`

4. `def self_conv(p): return np.convolve(p,p, mode='full')`
5. `def local_score(p,n): q=self_conv(p); return 2*n*float(q.max())`
6. `def make_weights(q, gamma_frac, floor_frac=1e-8):`

7. `def coverage(p,w):`
   If n==1 return [w[0]*p0?]. General:
```
conv = np.convolve(p, w[::-1], mode='full')
idx = 2*p.size-2 - np.arange(p.size)
return conv[idx]
```
   Complexity O(n*2n). For n=2535 once per iter okay. Could use FFT for h? But direct no cancellation (all nonneg) okay.
8. `def step_logh(p,w,alpha):`
   h=coverage(p,w); hmax=h.max(); if hmax<=0: return p.copy()
   floor_h = max(1e-300, hmax*1e-15)
   logh = np.log(np.maximum(h, floor_h))
   target = np.mean(logh)  # or median
   floor_p = p.max()*1e-15
   logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, floor_p))
   log_new = (1-alpha)*logp + alpha*(target - logh)
   # To prevent overflow, subtract max before exp
   log_new = log_new - np.max(log_new)
   new = np.exp(log_new)
   s=new.sum()
   if not finite or s==0: return p.copy()
   return new/s
```
   For alpha=1, uses old p only in floor, effectively p_new∝1/h normalized. Good.
   Maybe use median instead of mean to robust: `target=np.median(logh)`. Mean OK.

def multiplicative_optimize(start, n, budget_s, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
    p=normalize_nonneg(start)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=local_score(p,n)
    current_score=best_score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    alpha=0.25
    gamma_frac=0.15
    alphas=[1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1]
    reset_count=0; no_global=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # if too close, break
        # compute current q and weights
        q=self_conv(p); m=q.max(); current_score=2*n*m
        if current_score < best_score:
            best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score; no_global=0
        # make weights maybe refresh every few it? gamma anneal
        w=make_weights(q,gamma_frac)
        # try a few alphas, choose best candidate; to save convs, compute h once
        h=coverage(p,w)
        # generate candidates for alpha list; choose one with min score

            p=best_cand; current_score=best_cand_score

                best=p.copy(); best_score=best_cand_score; no_global=0
            # alpha adaptation: move towards tried that worked? We can't know which; just increase base alpha if accepted? set alphas scale? Maybe maintain `alpha_base`.

            # adjust gamma: cycle

                gamma_frac = 0.05

                noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.02, n))

        if verbose and it%100==0: print

LP phase implementation in detail:
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
    n = len(f); m=2*n-1
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
    diags=[]; offsets=[]
    for d in range(n):
        diags.append(np.full(n, f[d], dtype=np.float64))
        offsets.append(-d)
    return sp.diags(diags, offsets, shape=(m,n), format='csc')

def lp_direction(f, M, timeout=60):
    # f length n, M max(f*f)
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
    A=build_conv_matrix(f)
    c=-np.ones(n)
    b=np.full(2*n-1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    opts={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance': 1e-8, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance': 1e-8}
    if 'time_limit' in ...: opts['time_limit']=timeout
    res=so.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options=opts)
    if res.success: return np.maximum(res.x,0)
    # if not success but feasible? res.x maybe; if finite and satisfies? check
    if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
        # check constraints internally with matrix-vector? A @ res.x maybe; but building again. Could return if max <= M(1+1e-6)
        val = A.dot(res.x)
        if np.max(val) <= M*(1+1e-6) + 1e-9:
             return np.maximum(res.x,0)
    return None
```

Let's write `step_from_h` robust:
```
def step_from_h(p, h, alpha):
    n=p.size
    hmax = h.max()
    if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0:
        return p.copy()
    h_floor = max(1e-300, hmax*1e-14)
    logh = np.log(np.maximum(h, h_floor))
    target = float(np.median(logh))
    p_floor = p.max()*1e-14
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, p_floor))
    lognew = (1.0-alpha)*logp + alpha*(target - logh)
    mx = np.max(lognew)
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return p.copy()
    val = np.exp(lognew - mx)
    s = float(val.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return p.copy()
    return (val/s).astype(np.float64)
```
This recomputes logh/logp for each candidate. To avoid, precompute:
```
def prepare_log(p,h):
   hmax=...; logh=...; target=...; p_floor=...; logp=...
   return logp, logh, target
def step_from_log_movement(logp, logh, target, alpha): ...
```
But target and h floor. For multiple alphas and same p/h, we can precompute once in optimizer.

`make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(q, gamma_frac):
    m=q.max()
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
       return np.ones(q.shape)
    x = (q/m - 1.0)/max(gamma_frac,1e-6)
    x = np.maximum(x, -50.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    # Ensure not all zero
    w = w / w.max() (max=1)
    # Tiny floor to touch all constraints; relative tiny
    w = w + 1e-9
    return w
```
The weight sum not important. If gamma_frac large (e.g., 1), all weights ~e^-1 =>0.37; uniform-ish. If small, focus max.

Multiplicative optimizer with precomputed logs:
```
def multiplicative_optimize(p0,n, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p0)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=local_score(p,n)
    current_score=best_score
    rng=...
    alpha=0.35
    gamma_frac=0.1
    it=0; no_global=0; no_any=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # If best close to lower bound 1.0002 break? no.
       q=self_conv(p); m=q.max(); current_score=2.0*n*m
       if current_score < best_score:
           best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score; no_global=0; no_any=0
       if best_score <= 1.00001: break
       w=make_weights(q,gamma_frac)
       h=coverage(p,w)
       # precompute logp/logh
       p_floor=p.max()*1e-14; logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,p_floor))
       hmax=h.max()
       if not finite or hmax<=0: break? p=p/best?
       logh=np.log(np.maximum(h, max(1e-300,hmax*1e-14)))
       target=float(np.median(logh))
       # choose a few alphas: alpha, alpha/2, min(1, alpha*2) if alpha*2 !=?
       alphas=[]
       a=_alpha
       for a in (alpha, 0.5*alpha, min(1.0, 1.5*alpha)):
           if 0<a<=1 and a not in alphas: alphas.append(a)
       best_cand_sc=current_score; best_cand=None; best_a=None
       for a in alphas:
           cand = step_from_logs(logp, logh, target, a)
           sc = local_score(cand,n)
           if sc < best_cand_sc:
              best_cand_sc=sc; best_cand=cand; best_a=a
       if best_cand is not None and best_cand_sc < current_score - 1e-13:
           p=best_cand; current_score=best_cand_sc; no_any=0
           if current_score < best_score:
               best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score; no_global=0
           alpha = float(np.clip(alpha*1.08, 0.05, 1.0))
       else:
           no_any +=1
           alpha = float(max(0.05, alpha*0.7))
           # adjust gamma
           gamma_frac *= 1.4
           if gamma_frac > 2.0:
               gamma_frac = 0.05
               # restart from best with perturbation
               p = best.copy()
               if no_global > 30:
                   noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n))
                   p = p*noise
                   p=normalize_nonneg(p)
                   current_score=local_score(p,n)
                   if current_score < best_score: ...
               alphas? alpha=0.3

           # every 50 no_any try reverse blend
           if no_any % 50 == 0 and no_any>0:

               rev = normalize_nonneg(best[::-1]); cand = (1-rho)*best + rho*rev; normalize; sc eval; if sc<best update.
       it +=1
       if verbose and it%100==0: print...
    return best,best_score

def step_from_logs(logp, logh, target, alpha):

def lp_phase(best, best_score, bases, n, start_time, deadline, verbose=False):
    S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    count=0

So M=local_score(base,n) (score normalized) = max_f. Good.
        g = lp_direction(f, M)
        if g is None:

        # if best below lower or deadline break; also if no improvement? Can continue with other base.

LP timeout: We need set options. Let's define:
```
def lp_direction(f, M, timeout=90):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
    n=len(f); m=2*n-1
    diags=[]; offsets=[]
    max_f=max(f)
    for d in range(n):
       val=f[d]
       if abs(val)>1e-300:
          diags.append(np.full(n, val, dtype=np.float64))
          offsets.append(-d)
    if not diags: return None
    A=sp.diags(diags, offsets, shape=(m,n), format='csc')
    c=-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b=np.full(m, M, dtype=np.float64)
    options={'presolve': True}
    if timeout is not None:
       options['time_limit']=int(max(1, timeout))
       # maybe thread count
       options['thread_count']=1
    try:
       res=so.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options=options)
    except Exception as e:
       print('LP error', e); return None
    if res is not None and res.x is not None:
       x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
       if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
          x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
          # If not success, still check feasibility
          if res.success or np.max(A.dot(x)) <= M + max(1e-8, 1e-6*M):
             return x
    return None
```
But if `options['time_limit']` unsupported and linprog raises, catch? It catches exception and returns None, losing LP. To avoid, only set time_limit if method highs supports? We can omit to be safe. But risk timeout overrun. I think options unsupported are silently ignored by scipy? I check: SciPy linprog validates options? It may warn 'Parameters: time_limit' unknown? It may still work. To be safer, set only `{'presolve': True}`. We can still have LP run longer. But expected within. Let's set `time_limit` but if exception due unknown, catch and retry without? We can try with options then fail; but building A twice expensive. Better check `scipy.__version__`? Not. We can pass `options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': timeout}`; if raise, catch and return None. Risk no LP. I think Highs supports time_limit. Use.

Also set `options['output_flag']=False`? Not needed. Print warnings? Set `verbose`? Not.

Could reduce LP matrix rows/columns if f tail small? Not.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 45.0
    deadline = t0 + budget - reserve
    # choose n
    n = N_DEFAULT
    # Maybe adjust if budget smaller? if budget<600, n=1024
    if budget < 900:
        n=1024
    verbose = True
    print(f'ProSeq n={n} budget={budget}')
    # initial search until min(t0+ n? maybe 60s or deadline)
    init_deadline = min(t0 + 60.0, deadline)
    scored = initial_search(n, seed, init_deadline, verbose)
    if not scored: p_central = central_sequence(n); scored=[(local_score(p_central,n),0,p_central)]
    best_score, _, best = scored[0]
    print best.
    # Maybe use height_sequence_1 if it has length and score better? initial_search includes if len==n.
    # Phase 1 multiplicative for 1200 seconds max or until deadline-300
    phase_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 1200.0)
    if phase_deadline > t0+60:
        best,best_score = multiplicative_optimize(best,n,phase_deadline,seed,verbose)
    # Collect bases for LP: best, second best from initial, central
    bases=[]
    for item in scored[:3]:
       p=item[2]
       # avoid duplicates? not necessary
       bases.append(p.copy())
    # Add central
    bases.append(central_sequence(n))
    # LP phase if scipy and enough time
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 240:
        lp_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 900.0)
        best,best_score=lp_phase(best,best_score,bases,n,lp_deadline,verbose)
    # final multiplicative with remaining
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        best,best_score = multiplicative_optimize(best,n,deadline,seed+7,verbose)
    # cleanup best
    best=normalize_nonng(best)
    # Try final scaling maybe to sum = 1.0; could also scale to sum=10 to avoid underflow.
    # call evaluator
    try:
        final_score=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best])
        print('final evaluate', final_score)
        if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_score > best_score + 0.01:
            # fallback to central? if evaluate inf due weird
            # Actually if finite but worse than internal due? no.
            pass
    except Exception as e:
        print('eval fail',e)
    # ensure all nonnegative finite
    best = np.where(np.isfinite(best), best, 0.0)
    best = np.maximum(best, 0.0)
    if best.sum() <= 0:
       best = central_sequence(n)
    # maybe scale to sum=1 (already)
    return [float(x) for x in best]
```

Let's write robust `normalize_nonng`:
```
def normalize_nonng(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
    arr = arr / s
    # additional floor? not here to preserve zeros? For optimization floor. Output can have zeros.
    return arr
```
If input is list with bool? evaluate not; our arr numeric.

`central_sequence`:
```
def central_sequence(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=1: return np.ones(n)/n
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        a[i]=a[i-1]*((2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i))
    return normalize_nonng(a)
```
For n large, recurrence stable. To speed, vectorize log? Loop 2048 negligible.

```
def multiplicative_optimize(p0, n, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
    p = normalize_nonng(p0); best=p.copy(); best_score=local_score(p,n)
    current_score=best_score
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    alpha = 0.35
    gamma_frac = 0.1
    no_global = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # If remaining small, break after maybe one quick? no
        q = self_conv(p)
        m = float(q.max())
        current_score = 2.0*n*m
        if current_score < best_score:
            best = p.copy(); best_score = current_score; no_global=0
        if best_score <= 1.00005:
            break
        w = make_weights(q, gamma_frac)
        h = coverage(p, w)
        pmax = float(p.max()) if p.size else 1.0
        if pmax <=0: break
        p_floor = pmax*1e-14
        logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, p_floor))
        hmax = float(h.max())
        if hmax <=0 or not np.isfinite(hmax):
            h_floor = 1e-300
        else:
            h_floor = max(1e-300, hmax*1e-14)
        logh = np.log(np.maximum(h, h_floor))
        target = float(np.median(logh))
        # alphas: choose [alpha, 0.5alpha] and if alpha<1 and random alpha2? 
        alpha2 = float(min(1.0, alpha*1.8))
        candidates=[]
        for a in (alpha, 0.5*alpha, alpha2):
            if a > 0.01 and a <=1.2:
                # normalize alpha to <=1? if >1, formula (1-alpha) negative. Step could be too aggressive. Better cap 1.0.
                a = min(1.0, float(a))
                if a not in ... no lambda; use list check
                c = step_from_logs(logp, logh, target, a)
                # To save time, maybe not compute score for all if time? compute
                sc = local_score(c, n)
                ... choose best
        ...
```

      best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score; no_global=0

else:
   no_global += 1
   if no_global < 30:
       alpha = max(0.05, alpha*0.6)
       gamma_frac *= 1.3
   else:
       # restart from best with perturbation magnitude increasing
       strength = 0.05 + 0.02*(no_global//50)

       noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, n))

def build_conv_matrix_sparse(f):
    n=f.size; m=2*n-1
    diags=[]; offsets=[]; 
    for d in range(n):
        if abs(f[d]) > 1e-300:
            diags.append(np.full(n, f[d], dtype=np.float64))
            offsets.append(-d)
    return sp.diags(diags, offsets, shape=(m,n), format='csc')
```

For p length n, full convolution can be computed by:
```
def fft_convolve(a,b=None):
   if b is None: length=2*n-1; N=1
   while N < length: N <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
   if b is None: fb=fa
   else: fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
   out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, N)[:length].real
   return out
```

Let's write `initial_search` in detail:
```
def initial_search(n, seed, deadline, verbose=False):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    c = central_sequence(n)
    seqs = [c]
    # hseq
    hseq = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if hseq is not None:
        harr = np.asarray(hseq, dtype=np.float64)
        if harr.ndim==1 and harr.size>1 and np.all(np.isfinite(harr)):
            harr = np.maximum(harr,0.0)
            if harr.size != n:
                old = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,harr.size)
                new = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
                harr = np.interp(new, old, harr)
            harr = normalize_nonng(harr)
            seqs.append(harr)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n-1) if n > 1 else np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    betas = (-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0)
    for b in betas:
        f = np.exp(b*x)
        seqs.append(c*f)
    pows = (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0)
    us = (-0.5,-0.2,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0)
    for pw in pows:
        xp = np.power(x, pw)
        for u in us:
            f = 1.0 + u*xp
            if np.min(f) > 0:
                seqs.append(c*f)
    ramp = np.clip((x-0.5)/0.5, 0.0, 1.0)
    for t in (0.1,0.3,0.7,1.5,3.0):
        seqs.append(c + t*(c[::-1]*ramp))
    for s in (0.5,0.7,0.85,1.2,1.5):
        seqs.append(np.power(c, s))
    # random polynomial
    for r in range(12):
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=4)
        f = 1.0 + coeff[0]*x + coeff[1]*(x*x) + coeff[2]*(x*x*x)
        if np.min(f) > 0.0:
            seqs.append(c*f)
    scored=[]
    idx=0
    for s in seqs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        p=normalize_nonng(s)
        q=self_conv(p)
        sc=2.0*n*float(q.max())
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), idx, p.copy()))
        idx +=1
    if verbose:
        print('initial candidates', len(scored))
    scored.sort()
    return scored
```
`np.min(f) > 0` if f array. Good.

If deadline is small (t0+30), evaluating 50 convs okay. If less, break.

`local_score` may be used inside initial search; okay.

Multiplicative `step_from_logs` when alpha=0? Avoid. If alpha negative? no.
```
def step_from_logs(logp, logh, target, alpha):
    a = float(alpha)
    if a <= 0.0: return None
    lognew = (1.0 - a) * logp + a * (target - logh)
    mx = np.max(lognew)
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return None
    vals = np.exp(lognew - mx)
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return None
    return vals / s
```

`make_weights` if gamma_frac large, x maybe (small-1)/large; no underflow. Add floor.
```
def make_weights(q, gamma_frac):
    m = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0:
        return np.ones(q.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    g = max(float(gamma_frac), 1e-6)
    x = (q/m - 1.0) / g
    np.maximum(x, -50.0, out=x)
    w = np.exp(x)
    wmax = float(w.max())
    if wmax > 0 and np.isfinite(wmax):
        w = w / wmax
    else:
        w.fill(1.0)
    w += 1e-9
    return w
```
`np.maximum` with out modifies x; x is new array. Good.

`coverage` using `np.convolve`; if p,w large but nonnegative. `idx = (2*n - 2) - np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)`. Need if n large, intp.

Now `multiplicative_optimize` improvements:
At top, if `deadline` is not greater than current time, return. Use `time.monotonic()`.
```
def multiplicative_optimize(p0,n,deadline,seed=0,verbose=False):
    p = normalize_nonng(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    q0 = np.convolve(p,p,mode='full')
    best_score = float(2.0*n*np.max(q0))
    current_score = best_score
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    alpha = 0.35
    gamma_frac = 0.10
    no_global = 0
    iters=0
    last_print=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = np.convolve(p,p,mode='full')
        m = float(np.max(q))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0: break
        current_score = float(2.0*n*m)
        if current_score < best_score:
            best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score; no_global=0
        if best_score <= 1.00005: break
        w = make_weights(q, gamma_frac)
        h = coverage(p,w)
        pmax = float(p.max())
        if pmax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(pmax): break
        p_floor = pmax*1e-14
        logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, p_floor))
        hmax = float(h.max())
        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0:
            h_floor = 1e-300
        else:
            h_floor = max(1e-300, hmax*1e-14)
        logh = np.log(np.maximum(h, h_floor))
        target = float(np.median(logh))
        # candidate generation
        alphas=[]
        a1=alpha
        a2=0.5*alpha
        a3=min(1.0, 1.8*alpha)
        for a in (a1,a2,a3):
            a=float(a)
            if a > 0.02 and a <= 1.0:
                if a not in alphas:
                    alphas.append(a)
        best_cand=None; best_cand_score=current_score
        for a in alphas:
            cand = step_from_logs(logp, logh, target, a)
            if cand is None: continue
            q2 = np.convolve(cand,cand,mode='full')
            sc = float(2.0*n*np.max(q2))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand
        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < current_score - 1e-14:
            p=best_cand; current_score=best_cand_score
            if current_score < best_score:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score; no_global=0
            alpha = min(1.0, alpha*1.12)
        else:
            no_global += 1
            alpha = max(0.06, alpha*0.7)
            gamma_frac *= 1.35
            if gamma_frac > 1.5 or no_global > 60:
                # restart
                strength = 0.04 + 0.02*min(5, no_global//60)
                p = best.copy() * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, size=n))
                if no_global % 20 == 0:
                    p = 0.5*p + 0.5*normalize_nonng(p[::-1])
                p = normalize_nonng(p)
                current_score = float(2.0*n*np.max(np.convolve(p,p,mode='full')))
                if current_score < best_score:
                    best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score
                alpha=0.30
                gamma_frac=0.08
                no_global=0
        iters +=1
        if verbose and iters - last_print > 500:
            print('multi iter', iters, 'best', best_score, 'current', current_score)
            last_print=iters
    return best, best_score
```
Potential inefficiency: If no restart, gamma_frac increases over no_global, but we don't reset gamma when p improves. If p improves, gamma remains as is (maybe even after accepted multiple times, gamma could have grown from previous failures). Could reset gamma to 0.1 when best improves significantly? Not necessary. But if alpha grows and gamma large, weights uniform, maybe no progress. Good.

When we accept a candidate that is better than current but not best, no_global remains? We set no_global? In code above, if best_cand improves current, we do not reset no_global unless best_score improves. That may cause a restart while in a downhill move. Better set `no_global = 0` on any accepted current improvement? It indicates progress; restart less. But we also want global improvements. Let's set `progress = 0` for any accepted, and `stagnant` for no global? Simpler: If accepted (current score improved), set `stagnant = 0` (even if not global). If not accepted, stagnation++. Restart based on no improvement in score at all. But if we keep moving downhill and not improving global for long, maybe should restart eventually. Use two counters? Simpler: `stall=0` increments only when no candidate improves current. If accepted, `stall=0`. Global tracking only for best. Restart if stall>60. This avoids restarting during exploration. We'll implement `stall`.
```
stall=0
...
if accepted: p=...; stall=0; if sc<best: best=...
else: stall+=1; ... if stall>60: restart
```
Good.

In restart, if no_global? use `stall`.

Now, if alpha grows to 1 and steps accepted repeatedly but p oscillates (score may improve then later no), line search halves when no improvement. Good.

Potential issue: `np.convolve(cand,cand)` for each alpha; if alpha=1, cand might be extreme, but score finite. Good.

Could the candidate sum to zero due exp underflow? at least one val=1 after subtract max. Good.

Now, LP phase:
```
def lp_phase(best,best_score,bases,n,deadline,verbose=False):
    S0 = math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    order=0
    calls=0
    max_calls=8
    no_imp=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 30 and calls < max_calls:
        if not HAVE_SCIPY: break
        if not bases: break
        base = bases[order % len(bases)].copy()
        order +=1
        base = normalize_nonng(base)
        # floor first element to avoid unbounded
        bmax=float(base.max())
        base=np.maximum(base, bmax*1e-12)
        base=normalize_nonng(base)
        q_base=np.convolve(base,base, mode='full')
        M=float(2.0*n*np.max(q_base))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: continue
        f=base*S0
        timeout = max(20, int(min(180, (deadline - time.monotonic())*0.25)))
        g=lp_direction(f, M, timeout)
        calls +=1
        if g is None:
            if calls >= 3:
                break
            continue
        G=float(g.sum())
        if G <= 0 or not np.isfinite(G): continue
        g_norm = g/G
        # maybe normalize nonneg
        cand_list=[]
        cand_list.append(g_norm)
        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.5, 0.25):
            # blend with best? If t=1 same; include
            cand_list.append(np.maximum((1.0-t)*best + t*g_norm, 0.0))
        # blend with base
        for t in (0.5,0.8):
            cand_list.append(np.maximum((1.0-t)*base + t*g_norm,0.0))
        improved=False
        for cand in cand_list:
            p=normalize_nonng(cand)
            sc=float(2.0*n*np.max(np.convolve(p,p,mode='full')))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=float(sc); improved=True
                if verbose: print('LP improved', best_score)
        if not improved:
            no_imp +=1
            if no_imp > len(bases):
                break
        else:
            no_imp=0
    return best,best_score
```

          pass
    A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    cols=np.arange(n,dtype=np.intp)
    for d in range(n):
       val=float(f[d])
       if val != 0.0:

    return A

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences with small normalized auto-convolution peak.

The search maintains a probability vector p (sum = 1) and minimizes
    2*n*max_k (p*p)_k
where * denotes linear convolution.  It starts from several analytic /
interpolated initial points, applies a multiplicative coverage / mirror-descent
update derived from the active convolution constraints, and optionally uses
the previous state-of-art LP direction step (solve max sum g subject to
A(p) g <= max(p*p), g >= 0) via HiGHS if SciPy is available.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.sparse as sp
    import scipy.optimize as so
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


def normalize_nonng(a):
    """Return a finite nonnegative 1-D float array with sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    arr = arr / s
    return arr


def central_sequence(n):
    """
    The truncated inverse-square-root (central-binomial) sequence, normalized.
    Asymptotically gives value near pi/2.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    return normalize_nonng(a)


def max_conv(p):
    """Return max coefficient of the full self-convolution of p."""
    q = np.convolve(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), mode="full")
    return float(np.max(q))


def local_score(p, n):
    """Objective for a probability vector p: 2*n*max(p*p)."""
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * max_conv(p))


def make_weights(q, gamma_frac):
    """
    Smooth indicator of the active convolution constraints.
    Larger values of q get larger multiplicative weight.
    """
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(q.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    g = max(float(gamma_frac), 1e-6)
    x = (q / m - 1.0) / g
    x = np.maximum(x, -50.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    wmax = float(np.max(w))
    if np.isfinite(wmax) and wmax > 0.0:
        w = w / wmax
    else:
        w = np.ones_like(w)
    # Small floor keeps every constraint weakly present and avoids zero rows.
    w += 1e-9
    return w


def coverage(p, w):
    """
    h_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}, the forward-dual coverage vector.
    p has length n, w has length 2*n-1.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(w[0] * p[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    conv = np.convolve(p, w[::-1], mode="full")
    idx = (2 * n - 2) - np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    return np.asarray(conv[idx], dtype=np.float64)


def step_from_logs(logp, logh, target, alpha):
    """
    Multiplicative update:
        p_i <- p_i * (target / h_i)^alpha, normalized.
    """
    a = float(alpha)
    if not np.isfinite(a) or a <= 0.0:
        return None
    a = min(1.0, a)
    lognew = (1.0 - a) * logp + a * (target - logh)
    mx = np.max(lognew)
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return None
    vals = np.exp(lognew - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return vals / s


def multiplicative_optimize(p0, n, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    Adaptive multiplicative / mirror-descent search on log p.
    Returns best probability vector and its local score.
    """
    p = normalize_nonng(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    q0 = np.convolve(p, p, mode="full")
    best_score = float(2.0 * float(n) * np.max(q0))
    current_score = best_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    alpha = 0.35
    gamma_frac = 0.10
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -1000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = np.convolve(p, p, mode="full")
        m = float(np.max(q))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break
        current_score = float(2.0 * float(n) * m)

        if current_score < best_score:
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = current_score
            stall = 0

        if best_score <= 1.00005:
            break

        w = make_weights(q, gamma_frac)
        h = coverage(p, w)

        pmax = float(np.max(p))
        if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 0.0:
            break

        p_floor = pmax * 1e-14
        logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, p_floor))

        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
            h_floor = 1e-300
        else:
            h_floor = max(1e-300, hmax * 1e-14)
        logh = np.log(np.maximum(h, h_floor))
        target = float(np.median(logh))

        alphas = []
        a_candidates = (alpha, 0.5 * alpha, min(1.0, 1.8 * alpha))
        for aa in a_candidates:
            aa = float(aa)
            if aa > 0.02 and aa <= 1.0:
                ok = True
                for bb in alphas:
                    if abs(aa - bb) <= 1e-12:
                        ok = False
                        break
                if ok:
                    alphas.append(aa)

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_score = current_score
        for aa in alphas:
            cand = step_from_logs(logp, logh, target, aa)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            qc = np.convolve(cand, cand, mode="full")
            sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * np.max(qc))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_cand = cand

        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < current_score - 1e-14:
            p = best_cand
            current_score = best_cand_score
            stall = 0
            if current_score < best_score:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = current_score
            alpha = min(1.0, alpha * 1.12)
        else:
            stall += 1
            alpha = max(0.06, alpha * 0.7)
            gamma_frac *= 1.35

            if gamma_frac > 1.5 or stall > 60:
                strength = 0.04 + 0.02 * min(5, stall // 60)
                p = best.copy() * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, size=n))
                if stall % 20 == 0:
                    rev = normalize_nonng(p[::-1])
                    p = 0.5 * p + 0.5 * rev
                p = normalize_nonng(p)
                current_score = local_score(p, n)
                if current_score < best_score:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = current_score
                alpha = 0.30
                gamma_frac = 0.08
                stall = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and it - last_print > 500:
            print("  multiplicative iter", it, "best", best_score, "current", current_score)
            last_print = it

    return best, best_score


def initial_search(n, seed, deadline, verbose=False):
    """Generate and evaluate a small set of informed starting vectors."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    c = central_sequence(n)
    seqs = [c]

    hseq = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if hseq is not None:
        harr = np.asarray(hseq, dtype=np.float64)
        if harr.ndim == 1 and harr.size > 1:
            harr = np.where(np.isfinite(harr), harr, 0.0)
            harr = np.maximum(harr, 0.0)
            if harr.sum() > 0.0:
                if harr.size > 200000:
                    idx = np.linspace(0, harr.size - 1, 200000, dtype=np.int64)
                    harr = harr[idx]
                if harr.size != n:
                    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, harr.size)
                    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                    harr = np.interp(new, old, harr)
                seqs.append(normalize_nonng(harr))

    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n - 1)
    else:
        x = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    # Exponential tilts.
    for b in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        seqs.append(c * np.exp(b * x))

    # Polynomial tilts.
    for pw in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        xp = np.power(x, pw)
        for u in (-0.5, -0.2, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            f = 1.0 + u * xp
            if float(np.min(f)) > 0.0:
                seqs.append(c * f)

    # Add a windowed reversed inverse-sqrt tail.
    ramp = np.clip((x - 0.5) / 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)
    for t in (0.1, 0.3, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0):
        seqs.append(c + t * (c[::-1] * ramp))

    # Powers of the central profile.
    for s in (0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 1.2, 1.5):
        seqs.append(np.power(c, s))

    # Low-degree polynomial perturbations.
    for _ in range(12):
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=4)
        f = 1.0 + coeff[0] * x + coeff[1] * (x * x) + coeff[2] * (x * x * x)
        if float(np.min(f)) > 0.0:
            seqs.append(c * f)

    scored = []
    for i, s in enumerate(seqs):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        p = normalize_nonng(s)
        q = np.convolve(p, p, mode="full")
        sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * np.max(q))
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, i, p.copy()))

    scored.sort()
    if verbose:
        print("initial candidates:", len(scored))
        if len(scored):
            print("  best initial score:", scored[0][0])
    return scored


def build_convolution_matrix(f):
    """
    Build the full convolution matrix A(p) with rows k and columns i:
        A[k, i] = f_{k-i} if 0 <= k-i < n, else 0.
    A x = f * x.
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    n = f.size
    m = 2 * n - 1

    if HAVE_SCIPY:
        try:
            diags = []
            offsets = []
            for d in range(n):
                v = float(f[d])
                if v != 0.0:
                    diags.append(np.full(n, v, dtype=np.float64))
                    offsets.append(-d)
            if diags:
                return sp.diags(diags, offsets, shape=(m, n), format="csc")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Dense fallback.
    A = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    cols = np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    for d in range(n):
        v = float(f[d])
        if v != 0.0:
            A[d:d + n, cols] = v
    return A


def lp_direction(f, M, timeout=80):
    """
    Solve:
        maximize sum(g)
        subject to A(f) g <= M, g >= 0.
    Returns feasible g or None.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None

    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    M = float(M)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0 or n <= 0:
        return None

    try:
        A = build_convolution_matrix(f)
    except Exception:
        return None

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.full(m, M, dtype=np.float64)

    res = None
    try:
        opts = {
            "presolve": True,
            "time_limit": int(max(1, timeout)),
        }
        try:
            res = so.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method="highs", options=opts)
        except Exception:
            res = so.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method="highs")
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.shape[0] != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(x)) <= 0.0:
        return None

    # If the solver did not report success, still use it if feasible.
    try:
        vals = np.asarray(A.dot(x), dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if vals.size == m:
            tol = max(1e-8, 1e-7 * abs(M))
            if float(np.max(vals)) <= M + tol:
                return x
    except Exception:
        pass

    if getattr(res, "success", False):
        return x

    return None


def lp_phase(best, best_score, bases, n, deadline, verbose=False):
    """
    Repeatedly apply the LP direction iteration from the previous state of art.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best, best_score

    S0 = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    order = 0
    calls = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0 and calls < 6:
        if not bases:
            break

        base = bases[order % len(bases)].copy()
        order += 1

        base = normalize_nonng(base)
        bmax = float(np.max(base))
        if bmax > 0.0:
            base = np.maximum(base, bmax * 1e-12)
        base = normalize_nonng(base)

        bmax = float(np.max(base))
        if bmax <= 0.0:
            base = central_sequence(n)
            bmax = float(np.max(base))

        f = base * S0
        fmax = float(np.max(f))
        if not np.isfinite(fmax) or fmax <= 0.0:
            continue

        M = local_score(base, n)
        f = f / fmax
        M = M / fmax

        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            continue

        remaining = max(20.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        timeout = int(min(90.0, max(20.0, 0.25 * remaining)))
        g = lp_direction(f, M, timeout=timeout)
        calls += 1

        if g is None:
            if calls >= 3:
                break
            continue

        G = float(np.sum(g))
        if not np.isfinite(G) or G <= 0.0:
            continue

        g_norm = normalize_nonng(g)

        cand_list = [g_norm]
        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.5, 0.25):
            cand_list.append(np.maximum((1.0 - t) * best + t * g_norm, 0.0))
        for t in (0.8, 0.5):
            cand_list.append(np.maximum((1.0 - t) * base + t * g_norm, 0.0))

        improved = False
        for cand in cand_list:
            p = normalize_nonng(cand)
            sc = local_score(p, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                if verbose:
                    print("  LP improvement:", best_score)

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > len(bases) + 1:
                break

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.  Runs within budget_s and returns the best
    nonnegative sequence found.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 40.0
    deadline = t0 + budget - reserve

    # Moderate length keeps the final exact O(n^2) evaluator fast and gives
    # enough degrees of freedom to approach the target constant.
    n = 2048

    print(f"search_start n={n} budget={budget:.1f}s")

    # Initial informed search.
    init_budget = min(60.0, 0.03 * budget)
    init_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + init_budget)
    scored = initial_search(n, seed, init_deadline, verbose=True)
    if not scored:
        c0 = central_sequence(n)
        s0 = local_score(c0, n)
        scored = [(s0, 0, c0)]

    best_score = float(scored[0][0])
    best = scored[0][2].copy()
    print("initial best best_score:", best_score)

    # Phase 1: multiplicative descent from the best initial point.
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if time_left > 120.0:
        end1 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.30 * time_left)
        candidate, cand_score = multiplicative_optimize(best, n, end1, seed=seed, verbose=True)
        if cand_score < best_score:
            best = candidate
            best_score = cand_score
            print("phase1 best_score:", best_score)

    # Phase 2: second initial point, in case of a bad local basin.
    if len(scored) > 1 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 150.0:
        time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        end2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.12 * time_left)
        candidate, cand_score = multiplicative_optimize(scored[1][2], n, end2, seed=seed + 101, verbose=True)
        if cand_score < best_score:
            best = candidate
            best_score = cand_score
            print("phase2 best_score:", best_score)

    # Prepare bases for LP phase.
    bases = [best.copy()]
    for item in scored[:3]:
        bases.append(item[2].copy())
    bases.append(central_sequence(n))
    bases = [normalize_nonng(x) for x in bases]

    # Phase 3: LP direction iteration if available and if enough time remains.
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        endlp = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.35 * time_left)
        best, best_score = lp_phase(best, best_score, bases, n, endlp, verbose=True)
        print("after LP best_score:", best_score)

    # Phase 4: final multiplicative polish from the global best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        best, best_score = multiplicative_optimize(best, n, deadline, seed=seed + 997, verbose=True)
        print("after final polish best_score:", best_score)

    # Clean up final vector.
    best = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64)
    best = np.where(np.isfinite(best), best, 0.0)
    best = np.maximum(best, 0.0)
    bmax = float(np.max(best)) if best.size else 0.0
    if bmax > 0.0:
        # Keep all entries strictly positive at a negligible level to be safe.
        best = np.maximum(best, bmax * 1e-18)
    best = normalize_nonng(best)

    # Final safeguard: if something went catastrophically wrong, fall back.
    final_local = local_score(best, n)
    if not np.isfinite(final_local) or final_local > 2.05:
        best = central_sequence(n)
        final_local = local_score(best, n)

    out_list = [float(x) for x in best]

    # The harness provides evaluate_sequence; use it only for a final check / log.
    try:
        final_eval = evaluate_sequence(out_list)
        print("final local score:", final_local)
        print("final evaluate_sequence score:", final_eval)
        if (not np.isfinite(final_eval)) or final_eval > max(2.0, final_local + 0.05):
            # If the official evaluator disagrees badly, return a very safe fallback.
            out_list = [float(x) for x in central_sequence(n)]
    except Exception as exc:
        print("could not call evaluate_sequence:", exc)

    return out_list
```
